Here's the gist:

## Complaint Summary

**User (kursad-k)** runs LemonadeSDK — a local LLM server on Windows hosting 16 large GGUF models (DeepSeek-Qwen3-8B, Gemma-3-4b, etc.) on the same network. Two issues:

1. **Timeout during validation** — Moltis probes models sequentially by sending actual `POST /chat/completions` with `"ping"` to each model. The server needs to **load the model into VRAM** before it can respond. With large models, this easily exceeds the timeout.

2. **Unnecessary model loading churn** — The probe forces the server to unload the current model and load the next one for each attempt. For 8 large models that's ~100GB of I/O just to validate the provider. The user argues Moltis should just trust the `/models` list instead of probing each one.

## Is it real?

**Yes, this is a legitimate issue.** Looking at the code:

- **`probe_chat_completions()`** (`openai.rs:549`) sends an actual chat completion request (`"ping"`) per model — this forces the LLM server to load that model.
- **The timeout was 10s at the time of the report** (the logs show `elapsed_ms=10001`). It has since been bumped to **30s** (`VALIDATION_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SECS = 30`) and `MAX_TIMEOUTS` reduced from 2 to 1. That helps with genuinely slow servers but doesn't fix the core problem.
- **The fundamental issue remains**: for servers like LemonadeSDK/Ollama that can only hold one model in memory at a time, probing model-by-model causes a load/unload cycle for each attempt. With `VALIDATION_MAX_MODEL_PROBES = 8`, that's up to 8 model loads just to validate the provider key — each potentially taking 10-30+ seconds for large models.

The right fix would be to **probe only a single small model** (or better, use a lightweight endpoint like `GET /models` to confirm connectivity, and only fall back to a chat probe if that doesn't work). The current code already has `reorder_models_for_validation()` which likely tries smaller models first, but the sequential probing after the first success (`break` on line 2673) still means the *first* model attempted must fully load before succeeding.

> AGENT

The probe's only purpose is a **connectivity/credential check** — "can we actually talk to this endpoint and get a response from at least one model?"

It returns a simple `{ "valid": true, "models": [...] }` or `{ "valid": false, "error": "..." }`. The model list itself comes from the `/models` endpoint (already fetched before probing). The probe doesn't discover anything new — it just confirms the server can actually serve completions, not just list models.

So in practice it answers: "is this API key + base URL actually working end-to-end?" The model list is already known; the chat completion request is purely a smoke test.

For cloud providers (OpenAI, Anthropic) this makes sense — it validates the API key works. For local servers like LemonadeSDK/Ollama where there's no auth, the `/models` response already proves connectivity, making the chat probe redundant and expensive. The user's complaint is valid — a `GET /models` success should be sufficient for custom OpenAI-compatible providers, or at minimum probe only one model rather than cycling through up to 8.

## Summary of Web UI Provider Setup and Validation Flow

Based on my thorough analysis of the Moltis codebase, here's what I found:

### 1. JavaScript Files Handling Provider Configuration

**Main Files:**
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/web/src/assets/js/page-providers.js` - Lists configured providers and models on the Providers page
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/web/src/assets/js/providers.js` - Modal for adding new providers with validation flow
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/web/src/assets/js/provider-validation.js` - Core validation logic
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/web/src/assets/js/provider-validation-progress.js` - Progress tracking for validation operations

### 2. Validation Flow Triggered from UI

**The "Save & Validate" Button:**
- Located in the API Key form (line 583 in `providers.js`)
- Button text: `"Save & Validate"`
- When clicked, triggers `validateProviderKey()` function with:
  - Provider name
  - API key
  - Base URL (if applicable, for custom endpoints)
  - Model ID (for BYOM providers)
  - Request ID (for tracking validation progress)

**Validation RPC Call:**
```javascript
// From provider-validation.js, line 81:
var res = await sendRpc("providers.validate_key", payload);
```

**Response Handling:**
- Returns `{ valid: true, models?: [...] }` or `{ valid: false, error?: string }`
- If valid, shows model selector to choose which models to save
- If invalid, displays humanized error message and allows retry

### 3. HTML Elements Related to Provider Setup

**Modal Structure (from modals.js):**
- Modal ID: `providerModal`
- Title ID: `providerModalTitle`
- Body ID: `providerModalBody`
- Close Button ID: `providerModalClose`

**Form Elements Created Dynamically:**
- Provider selection list (displayed when modal opens)
- API Key input field
- Endpoint input field (for OpenAI-compatible providers)
- Model ID input field (for BYOM providers like Venice)
- Error panel (class: `alert-error-text`)
- Validation progress bar (class: `download-progress`)
- Progress text display (class: `text-xs text-[var(--muted)]`)

**Model Selector Form:**
- Search input for filtering models
- "Select All" / "Deselect All" button
- Model cards (searchable, clickable)
- Status display showing selected model count

### 4. API Endpoints for Provider Validation

**RPC Method: `providers.validate_key`**
- Handler: `crates/provider-setup/src/lib.rs` (line 2310)
- Scope: WRITE_METHODS (requires write permission)

**Flow:**
1. `validateProviderKey()` calls `sendRpc("providers.validate_key", payload)`
2. Backend processes validation asynchronously
3. Emits progress events via WebSocket on topic: `"providers.validate.progress"`
4. Progress events mapped to UI updates via `bindValidationProgressEvents()`

**Additional Methods:**
- `providers.save_key` - Persists validated credentials
- `providers.available` - Lists available provider configurations
- `models.test` - Tests a single model (retry mechanism with 40 attempts, 250ms delay)
- `models.detect_supported` - Detects supported models for provider validation

### 5. Validation Progress UI Components

**Progress Events Phases:**
- `"start"` → 8% progress
- `"candidates_discovered"` → 24% progress (shows model count)
- `"probe_started"` / `"probe_failed"` / `"probe_timeout"` → 24-86% (incremental per model probed)
- `"probe_succeeded"` → 94% progress
- `"complete"` → 100% progress
- `"error"` → 98% progress

**Visual Feedback:**
- Progress bar with `.download-progress-bar` (width animates 0-100%)
- Text messages update in real-time
- Validation hint text: "Validation can take up to 30 seconds for some providers."
- Running hint: "Validating models... this can take up to 30 seconds."

### 6. Error Handling & Humanization

**Error Messages (from provider-validation.js, lines 20-58):**
- Invalid API key → "Invalid API key. Please double-check and try again."
- Forbidden (403) → "Your API key doesn't have access. Check your account permissions."
- Rate limited → "Rate limited by the provider. Wait a moment and try again."
- Timeout → "Connection timed out. Check your endpoint URL and try again."
- Connection refused → "Connection refused. Make sure the provider endpoint is running and reachable."
- DNS errors → "Could not resolve the endpoint address. Check the URL and try again."
- 404 → "Model not found at this endpoint. Make sure it is installed and try again."

### 7. No Explicit "Test" or "Probe" Button

There is **no separate test/probe button** in the provider setup UI. Validation happens:
1. Automatically when saving a provider (via "Save & Validate" button)
2. After credentials are entered, the system validates by probing candidate models
3. The first model test happens as a connectivity check in the final save step (line 886)

The validation is integrated into the save flow, not a separate action.

### Key Files Reference

1. **Frontend UI & Logic:**
   - `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/web/src/assets/js/providers.js`
   - `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/web/src/assets/js/provider-validation.js`

   - `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/kindhearted-pisces/crates/gateway/src/provider_setup.rs`

## Summary

I've completed a thorough exploration of the error handling flow from LLM providers through the gateway to the web UI during chat requests. Here's what I found:

### Error Flow Architecture

**1. Provider → Gateway (Error Capture)**
- Location: `/crates/chat/src/lib.rs` (lines ~7305-7326)
- When the agent runner encounters an error, it's caught in `run_with_tools` as `AgentRunError`
- The error string is converted to a structured error object using `parse_chat_error()`

**2. Chat Error Parser (Structured Error Generation)**
- Location: `/crates/chat/src/chat_error.rs`
- Function: `parse_chat_error(raw: &str, provider_name: Option<&str>) -> Value`
- Intelligently parses raw error strings and produces structured JSON with:
  - `type`: error classification (e.g., `rate_limit_exceeded`, `auth_error`, `server_error`, `unsupported_model`, `max_iterations_reached`)
  - `icon`: emoji indicator
  - `title`: user-facing title
  - `detail`: detailed message
  - `title_key` / `detail_key`: i18n translation keys
  - Optional fields: `provider`, `resetsAt`, `retryAfterMs`, `detail_params`, `canContinue`

**Recognized Error Types:**
- Usage limit reached
- Rate limit exceeded (429, with retry-after parsing)
- Billing exhausted / insufficient quota
- Auth errors (401, 403)
- Server errors (500+)
- Model not found (404)
- Unsupported model (detects audio/image models probed via chat endpoints)
- Max iterations reached (with continue button)
- Generic fallback for unknown errors

**3. Gateway Error Broadcasting**
- Location: `/crates/gateway/src/server.rs` (line 1453)
- The gateway wires the error parser into `LiveProviderSetupService` via:
  ```rust
  .with_error_parser(crate::chat_error::parse_chat_error)
  ```
- Structured error is broadcast as `ChatErrorBroadcast` payload over WebSocket to the client
- Field: `error: Value` contains the full structured error object

**4. Provider Setup Error Parser (for validation)**
- Location: `/crates/provider-setup/src/lib.rs` (lines 1282-1288)
- The setup service uses the same parser during provider validation
- During model probing, errors are parsed and fed back to the UI as validation progress events
- Unsupported models are tracked separately and retried with other models

**5. Web UI Error Rendering**
- Location: `/crates/web/src/assets/js/websocket.js` (line 857)
- Handler: `handleChatError(p, isActive, isChatPage, eventSession)`
- Checks if error has `p.error?.title` (structured error) or falls back to `p.message` (plain text)
- Calls `localizeStructuredError()` to apply i18n translations

- Location: `/crates/web/src/assets/js/chat-ui.js` (line 136)
- Function: `chatAddErrorCard(err)`
- Renders error with:
  - Icon (from `err.icon`)
  - Title (localized via `title_key`)
  - Detail (localized via `detail_key` with `detail_params`)
  - Provider name (if available)
  - Countdown timer (if `resetsAt` present, e.g., for rate limits)
  - Continue button (if `canContinue` is true, for max iterations)

- Location: `/crates/web/src/assets/js/helpers.js` (lines 253-259)
- Function: `localizeStructuredError(error)`
- Translates `title_key` and `detail_key` using i18n system
- Falls back to English defaults if translation missing

### Key Features

**Error Classification Rules** (from `chat_error.rs`):
- Extracts HTTP status codes from error messages (401, 403, 404, 429, 500+)
- Parses embedded JSON error objects
- Detects `usage_limit_reached`, `rate_limit_exceeded`, `insufficient_quota` types
- Parses retry-after delays from headers or message text
- Identifies unsupported models based on keywords ("not supported", "not a chat model", etc.)
- Handles audio/image model detection when probed via chat endpoints

**Rate Limit Handling**:
- Extracts `resets_at` timestamps (converted to milliseconds for frontend)
- Extracts `retry_after_ms` from various header formats
- Frontend displays countdown timer until reset

**Provider Visibility**:
- All structured errors include the provider name for debugging
- Helps users understand which provider failed

**Localization**:
- All user-facing error titles/details are keyed for translation
- Fallback English text always available
- Translation keys like `errors:chat.rateLimited.title`, `errors:chat.authError.detail`

### Files to Reference

1. **Error Parsing**: `/crates/chat/src/chat_error.rs` (696 lines, well-tested)
2. **Gateway Integration**: `/crates/gateway/src/server.rs` (line 1453)
3. **WebSocket Handler**: `/crates/web/src/assets/js/websocket.js` (handleChatError function)
4. **Error UI Rendering**: `/crates/web/src/assets/js/chat-ui.js` (chatAddErrorCard function)
5. **Error Localization**: `/crates/web/src/assets/js/helpers.js` (localizeStructuredError function)
6. **Provider Setup**: `/crates/provider-setup/src/lib.rs` (error_parser field in LiveProviderSetupService)

### Current Error Coverage

The system already handles:
- Network errors with HTTP status codes
- API-specific errors from OpenAI, Anthropic, GitHub Copilot, etc.
- Authentication/authorization failures
- Rate limiting with retry guidance
- Billing/quota exhaustion
- Model availability issues
- Agent loop iteration limits with continue option

Clicking it shows a confirmation dialog: *"This will send a test message to one model, which may use tokens or trigger model loading on local servers. Continue?"* On confirm, it probes a single model and shows the result.

**3. Keep the probe infrastructure** — The `probe()` method and progress UI stay, they just move from the mandatory save flow to the optional test button.

     1→// ── LLMs page (Preact + HTM + Signals) ──────────────────────
     2→
     3→import { signal } from "@preact/signals";
     4→import { html } from "htm/preact";
     5→import { render } from "preact";
     6→import { useEffect, useState } from "preact/hooks";
     7→import { onEvent } from "./events.js";
     8→import { modelVersionScore, sendRpc } from "./helpers.js";
     9→import { t } from "./i18n.js";
    10→import { fetchModels } from "./models.js";
    11→import { updateNavCount } from "./nav-counts.js";
    12→import { openModelSelectorForProvider, openProviderModal } from "./providers.js";
    13→import { connected } from "./signals.js";
    14→import * as S from "./state.js";
    15→import { ConfirmDialog, requestConfirm } from "./ui.js";
    16→
    17→var configuredModels = signal([]);
    18→var providerMetaSig = signal(new Map());
    19→var loading = signal(false);
    20→var detectingModels = signal(false);
    21→var detectSummary = signal(null);
    22→var detectError = signal("");
    23→var detectProgress = signal(null);
    24→var deletingProvider = signal("");
    25→var providerActionError = signal("");
    26→
    27→function countUniqueProviders(models) {
    28→	return new Set(models.map((m) => m.provider)).size;
    29→}
    30→
    31→function progressFromPayload(payload) {
    32→	return {
    33→		total: payload?.total || 0,
    34→		checked: payload?.checked || 0,
    35→		supported: payload?.supported || 0,
    36→		unsupported: payload?.unsupported || 0,
    37→		errors: payload?.errors || 0,
    38→	};
    39→}
    40→
    41→function handleModelsUpdatedEvent(payload) {
    42→	if (!payload?.phase) return;
    43→	if (payload.phase === "start") {
    44→		detectingModels.value = true;
    45→		detectError.value = "";
    46→		detectSummary.value = null;
    47→		detectProgress.value = progressFromPayload(payload);
    48→		return;
    49→	}
    50→	if (payload.phase === "progress") {
    51→		detectingModels.value = true;
    52→		detectProgress.value = progressFromPayload(payload);
    53→		return;
    54→	}
    55→	if (payload.phase === "complete") {
    56→		detectingModels.value = false;
    57→		if (payload.summary) {
    58→			detectSummary.value = payload.summary;
    59→			detectProgress.value = progressFromPayload(payload.summary);
    60→		}
    61→		return;
    62→	}
    63→	if (payload.phase === "cancelled") {
    64→		detectingModels.value = false;
    65→		detectError.value = t("providers:detectionCancelled");
    66→		if (payload.summary) {
    67→			detectSummary.value = payload.summary;
    68→			detectProgress.value = progressFromPayload(payload.summary);
    69→		}
    70→		return;
    71→	}
    72→	if (payload.phase === "error") {
    73→		detectingModels.value = false;
    74→		detectError.value = payload.error || t("providers:modelDetectionFailed");
    75→	}
    76→}
    77→
    78→function fetchProviders() {
    79→	loading.value = true;
    80→	return Promise.all([sendRpc("models.list_all", {}), sendRpc("providers.available", {})])
    81→		.then(([modelsRes, providersRes]) => {
    82→			loading.value = false;
    83→			var providerMeta = new Map();
    84→			var configuredProviders = [];
    85→			if (providersRes?.ok) {
    86→				configuredProviders = (providersRes.payload || []).filter(
    87→					(configuredProvider) => configuredProvider.configured,
    88→				);
    89→				for (var providerMetaEntry of providersRes.payload || []) {
    90→					providerMeta.set(providerMetaEntry.name, providerMetaEntry);
    91→				}
    92→			}
    93→			providerMetaSig.value = providerMeta;
    94→
    95→			var models = [];
    96→			if (modelsRes?.ok) {
    97→				models = (modelsRes.payload || []).map((m) => ({
    98→					...m,
    99→					providerDisplayName: providerMeta.get(m.provider)?.displayName || m.provider,
   100→					authType: providerMeta.get(m.provider)?.authType || "api-key",
   101→				}));
   102→			}
   103→
   104→			// Include configured providers that don't currently expose a model.
   105→			var modelProviders = new Set(models.map((m) => m.provider));
   106→			var providerOnlyRows = [];
   107→			providerOnlyRows = configuredProviders
   108→				.filter((providerWithoutModels) => !modelProviders.has(providerWithoutModels.name))
   109→				.map((providerWithoutModels) => ({
   110→					id: `provider:${providerWithoutModels.name}`,
   111→					provider: providerWithoutModels.name,
   112→					displayName: providerWithoutModels.displayName,
   113→					providerDisplayName: providerWithoutModels.displayName,
   114→					providerOnly: true,
   115→					authType: providerWithoutModels.authType,
   116→				}));
   117→
   118→			configuredModels.value = [...models, ...providerOnlyRows];
   119→			updateNavCount("providers", countUniqueProviders(configuredModels.value));
   120→		})
   121→		.catch(() => {
   122→			loading.value = false;
   123→		});
   124→}
   125→
   126→async function runDetectAllModels() {
   127→	if (!connected.value || detectingModels.value) return;
   128→	detectingModels.value = true;
   129→	detectSummary.value = null;
   130→	detectError.value = "";
   131→	detectProgress.value = null;
   132→
   133→	try {
   134→		// Phase 1: show current full list first before probing.
   135→		await Promise.all([fetchModels(), fetchProviders()]);
   136→		await new Promise((resolve) => {
   137→			requestAnimationFrame(resolve);
   138→		});
   139→
   140→		var res = await sendRpc("models.detect_supported", {});
   141→		if (!res?.ok) {
   142→			detectError.value = res?.error?.message || t("providers:failedToDetectModels");
   143→			detectingModels.value = false;
   144→			return;
   145→		}
   146→		if (res.payload?.skipped) {
   147→			detectingModels.value = false;
   148→			return;
   149→		}
   150→		detectSummary.value = res.payload || null;
   151→		detectProgress.value = progressFromPayload(res.payload);
   152→		await Promise.all([fetchModels(), fetchProviders()]);
   153→		var p = detectProgress.value;
   154→		if (!p || p.total === 0 || p.checked >= p.total) {
   155→			detectingModels.value = false;
   156→		}
   157→	} catch (_err) {
   158→		detectingModels.value = false;
   159→	}
   160→}
   161→
   162→async function cancelDetection() {
   163→	var res = await sendRpc("models.cancel_detect", {});
   164→	if (!res?.ok) {
   165→		detectError.value = res?.error?.message || t("providers:modelDetectionFailed");
   166→	}
   167→}
   168→
   169→function groupProviderRows(models, metaMap) {
   170→	var groups = new Map();
   171→	for (var row of models) {
   172→		var key = row.provider;
   173→		if (!groups.has(key)) {
   174→			groups.set(key, {
   175→				provider: key,
   176→				providerDisplayName: row.providerDisplayName || row.displayName || key,
   177→				authType: row.authType || "api-key",
   178→				selectedModel: metaMap?.get(key)?.model || null,
   179→				models: [],
   180→			});
   181→		}
   182→		var groupEntry = groups.get(key);
   183→		if (!row.providerOnly) {
   184→			groupEntry.models.push(row);
   185→		}
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	var result = Array.from(groups.values());
   189→	result.sort((a, b) => {
   190→		var aOrder = metaMap?.get(a.provider)?.uiOrder;
   191→		var bOrder = metaMap?.get(b.provider)?.uiOrder;
   192→		var hasAOrder = Number.isFinite(aOrder);
   193→		var hasBOrder = Number.isFinite(bOrder);
   194→		if (hasAOrder && hasBOrder && aOrder !== bOrder) return aOrder - bOrder;
   195→		if (hasAOrder && !hasBOrder) return -1;
   196→		if (!hasAOrder && hasBOrder) return 1;
   197→		return a.providerDisplayName.localeCompare(b.providerDisplayName);
   198→	});
   199→	for (var providerGroup of result) {
   200→		providerGroup.models.sort((a, b) => {
   201→			// Preferred > recommended > newest date > highest version number > alpha.
   202→			var aPref = a.preferred ? 1 : 0;
   203→			var bPref = b.preferred ? 1 : 0;
   204→			if (aPref !== bPref) return bPref - aPref;
   205→			var aRec = a.recommended ? 1 : 0;
   206→			var bRec = b.recommended ? 1 : 0;
   207→			if (aRec !== bRec) return bRec - aRec;
   208→			var aTime = a.createdAt || 0;
   209→			var bTime = b.createdAt || 0;
   210→			if (aTime !== bTime) return bTime - aTime;
   211→			var aVer = modelVersionScore(a.id);
   212→			var bVer = modelVersionScore(b.id);
   213→			if (aVer !== bVer) return bVer - aVer;
   214→			return (a.displayName || a.id).localeCompare(b.displayName || b.id);
   215→		});
   216→	}
   217→	return result;
   218→}
   219→
   220→var DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MODELS = 3;
   221→
   222→function ProviderSection(props) {
   223→	var group = props.group;
   224→	var [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
   225→	var hasMore = group.models.length > DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MODELS;
   226→	var visibleModels = expanded || !hasMore ? group.models : group.models.slice(0, DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MODELS);
   227→	var hiddenCount = group.models.length - DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MODELS;
   228→
   229→	function onDeleteProvider() {
   230→		if (deletingProvider.value) return;
   231→		requestConfirm(t("providers:removeProviderConfirm", { name: group.providerDisplayName })).then((yes) => {
   232→			if (!yes) return;
   233→			deletingProvider.value = group.provider;
   234→			providerActionError.value = "";
   235→			sendRpc("providers.remove_key", { provider: group.provider })
   236→				.then((res) => {
   237→					if (res?.ok) {
   238→						configuredModels.value = configuredModels.value.filter((entry) => entry.provider !== group.provider);
   239→						fetchModels();
   240→						fetchProviders();
   241→						return;
   242→					}
   243→					providerActionError.value = res?.error?.message || t("providers:failedToDeleteProvider");
   244→				})
   245→				.catch(() => {
   246→					providerActionError.value = t("providers:failedToDeleteProvider");
   247→				})
   248→				.finally(() => {
   249→					deletingProvider.value = "";
   250→				});
   251→		});
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	function onToggleModel(model) {
   255→		var method = model.disabled ? "models.enable" : "models.disable";
   256→		sendRpc(method, { modelId: model.id }).then((res) => {
   257→			if (res?.ok) {
   258→				providerActionError.value = "";
   259→				configuredModels.value = configuredModels.value.map((entry) =>
   260→					entry.id === model.id ? { ...entry, disabled: !model.disabled } : entry,
   261→				);
   262→				fetchModels();
   263→				fetchProviders();
   264→			} else {
   265→				providerActionError.value = res?.error?.message || t("providers:failedToUpdateModel");
   266→			}
   267→		});
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	function onSelectModels() {
   271→		openModelSelectorForProvider(group.provider, group.providerDisplayName);
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	return html`<div id=${`provider-${group.provider}`} class="max-w-form py-1">
   275→		<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
   276→			<div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
   277→				<h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--text-strong)] truncate">${group.providerDisplayName}</h3>
   278→				<span class="provider-item-badge ${group.authType}">
   279→					${group.authType === "oauth" ? t("providers:oauth") : group.authType === "local" ? t("providers:local") : t("providers:apiKey")}
   280→				</span>
   281→			</div>
   282→			<div class="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
   283→				${group.models.length > 0 ? html`<button class="provider-btn provider-btn-secondary provider-btn-sm" onClick=${onSelectModels}>${t("providers:preferredModels.button")}</button>` : null}
   284→				<button
   285→					class="provider-btn provider-btn-danger provider-btn-sm"
   286→					disabled=${deletingProvider.value === group.provider}
   287→					onClick=${onDeleteProvider}
   288→				>
   289→					${deletingProvider.value === group.provider ? t("common:status.deleting") : t("common:actions.delete")}
   290→				</button>
   291→			</div>
   292→		</div>
   293→		<div class="mt-2 border-b border-[var(--border)]"></div>
   294→		${
   295→			group.models.length === 0
   296→				? html`<div class="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">${t("providers:noActiveModels")}</div>`
   297→				: html`<div class="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
   298→					${visibleModels.map(
   299→						(model) => html`<div key=${model.id} class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 py-1">
   300→							<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
   301→								<div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
   302→									<div class="text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-strong)] truncate">${model.displayName || model.id}</div>
   303→									${model.preferred ? html`<span class="recommended-badge">${t("providers:preferred")}</span>` : null}
   304→									${model.unsupported ? html`<span class="provider-item-badge warning" title=${model.unsupportedReason || t("providers:modelNotSupported")}>${t("providers:unsupported")}</span>` : null}
   305→									${model.supportsTools ? null : html`<span class="provider-item-badge warning">${t("providers:chatOnly")}</span>`}
   306→									${model.disabled ? html`<span class="provider-item-badge muted">${t("providers:disabled")}</span>` : null}
   307→								</div>
   308→								<div class="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] font-mono opacity-75">${model.id}</div>
   309→								${model.unsupported && model.unsupportedReason ? html`<div class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--danger,#ef4444)]">${model.unsupportedReason}</div>` : null}
   310→								${model.createdAt ? html`<time class="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] opacity-60 block" data-epoch-ms=${model.createdAt * 1000} data-format="year-month"></time>` : null}
   311→							</div>
   312→							<button class="provider-btn provider-btn-secondary provider-btn-sm" onClick=${() => onToggleModel(model)}>
   313→								${model.disabled ? t("common:actions.enable") : t("common:actions.disable")}
   314→							</button>
   315→						</div>`,
   316→					)}
   317→					${

   321→						onClick=${() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
   322→					>${expanded ? t("providers:showFewerModels") : t("providers:showAllModels", { count: hiddenCount })}</button>`

   329→
   330→function ProvidersPage() {
   331→	useEffect(() => {
   332→		if (connected.value) fetchProviders();
   333→		var offModelsUpdated = onEvent("models.updated", handleModelsUpdatedEvent);

   342→	var progressValue = detectProgress.value || { total: 0, checked: 0, supported: 0, unsupported: 0, errors: 0 };
   343→	var progressPercent = progressValue.total > 0 ? Math.round((progressValue.checked / progressValue.total) * 100) : 0;
   344→
   345→	return html`
   346→		<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 p-4 gap-4 overflow-y-auto">
   347→				<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
   348→					<h2 id="providersTitle" class="text-lg font-medium text-[var(--text-strong)]">${t("providers:title")}</h2>
   349→					<button
   350→						id="providersAddLlmBtn"
   351→						data-testid="providers-add-llm"
   352→						class="provider-btn"
   353→						onClick=${() => {
   354→							if (connected.value) openProviderModal();
   355→						}}
   356→					>
   357→						${t("providers:addLlm")}
   358→					</button>
   359→					<button
   360→						id="providersDetectModelsBtn"
   361→						data-testid="providers-detect-models"
   362→						class="provider-btn provider-btn-secondary"
   363→						disabled=${!connected.value || detectingModels.value}
   364→						onClick=${runDetectAllModels}
   365→					>
   366→						${detectingModels.value ? t("providers:detectingModels") : t("providers:detectAllModels")}
   367→					</button>
   368→				</div>
   369→				<p class="text-xs text-[var(--muted)] leading-relaxed max-w-form" style="margin:0;">
   370→					${t("providers:description")}
   371→				</p>
   372→				${
   373→					detectError.value || providerActionError.value
   374→						? html`<div class="text-xs text-[var(--danger,#ef4444)] max-w-form">${detectError.value || providerActionError.value}</div>`
   375→						: null
   376→				}
   377→				${
   378→					detectingModels.value
   379→						? html`<div class="max-w-form">
   380→							<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
   381→								<div class="flex-1 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-sm border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface2)]">
   382→									<div
   383→										class="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] transition-all duration-150"
   384→										style=${`width:${progressPercent}%;`}
   385→									></div>
   386→								</div>
   387→								<button
   388→									class="provider-btn provider-btn-danger provider-btn-sm"
   389→									onClick=${cancelDetection}
   390→								>
   391→									${t("providers:stopDetection")}
   392→								</button>
   393→							</div>
   394→							<div class="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
   395→								${t("providers:probingModels", { checked: progressValue.checked, total: progressValue.total, pct: progressPercent })}
   396→							</div>
   397→						</div>`
   398→						: detectSummary.value
   399→							? html`<div class="text-xs text-[var(--muted)] max-w-form">
   400→								${t("providers:detectSummary", { supported: detectSummary.value.supported || 0, unsupported: detectSummary.value.unsupported || 0, total: detectSummary.value.total || 0 })}
   401→							</div>`
   402→							: null
   403→				}
   404→
   405→				${(() => {
   406→					var groups = groupProviderRows(configuredModels.value, providerMetaSig.value);
   407→					if (loading.value && configuredModels.value.length === 0) {
   408→						return html`<div id="providersLoadingState" class="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">${t("common:status.loading")}</div>`;
   409→					}
   410→					if (configuredModels.value.length === 0) {
   411→						return html`<div id="providersEmptyState" data-testid="providers-empty-state" class="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" style="padding:12px 0;">${t("providers:noProvidersConfigured")}</div>`;
   412→					}
   413→					return html`<div id="providersConfiguredList" data-testid="providers-configured-list" style="max-width:600px;">
   414→						${
   415→							groups.length > 1
   416→								? html`<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-3">
   417→							${groups.map(
   418→								(g) => html`<button
   419→								key=${g.provider}
   420→								class="text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface)] text-[var(--muted)] hover:text-[var(--text)] hover:border-[var(--border-strong)] cursor-pointer"
   421→								onClick=${() => {
   422→									var el = document.getElementById(`provider-${g.provider}`);
   423→									if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
   424→								}}
   425→							>${g.providerDisplayName}<span class="ml-1 opacity-60">${g.models.length}</span></button>`,
   426→							)}
   427→						</div>`
   428→								: null
   429→						}
   430→						<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;margin-bottom:12px;">
   431→							${groups.map((g) => html`<${ProviderSection} key=${g.provider} group=${g} />`)}
   432→						</div>
   433→					</div>`;
   434→				})()}
   435→			</div>
   436→		<${ConfirmDialog} />
   437→		`;
   438→}
   439→
   440→var _providersContainer = null;
   441→
   442→export function initProviders(container) {
   443→	_providersContainer = container;
   444→	container.style.cssText = "flex-direction:column;padding:0;overflow:hidden;";
   445→	render(html`<${ProvidersPage} />`, container);
   446→}
   447→
   448→export function teardownProviders() {
   449→	S.setRefreshProvidersPage(null);
   450→	if (_providersContainer) render(null, _providersContainer);
   451→	_providersContainer = null;
   452→}

     1→import { sendRpc } from "./helpers.js";
     2→
     3→const MODEL_SERVICE_NOT_CONFIGURED = "model service not configured";
     4→const MODEL_TEST_RETRY_ATTEMPTS = 40;
     5→const MODEL_TEST_RETRY_DELAY_MS = 250;
     6→
     7→function firstProbeFailure(payload) {
     8→	var results = Array.isArray(payload?.results) ? payload.results : [];
     9→	var failed = results.find((r) => r?.status === "error" || r?.status === "unsupported");
    10→	if (!failed) return null;
    11→	if (typeof failed.error === "string" && failed.error.trim()) {
    12→		return failed.error.trim();
    13→	}
    14→	return null;
    15→}
    16→
    17→/**
    18→ * Map raw error strings to user-friendly messages.
    19→ */
    20→export function humanizeProbeError(error) {
    21→	if (!error || typeof error !== "string") return error;
    22→	var lower = error.toLowerCase();
    23→
    24→	if (
    25→		lower.includes("401") ||
    26→		lower.includes("unauthorized") ||
    27→		lower.includes("invalid api key") ||
    28→		lower.includes("invalid x-api-key")
    29→	) {
    30→		return "Invalid API key. Please double-check and try again.";
    31→	}
    32→	if (lower.includes("403") || lower.includes("forbidden")) {
    33→		return "Your API key doesn't have access. Check your account permissions.";
    34→	}
    35→	if (lower.includes("permission")) {
    36→		return error;
    37→	}
    38→	if (lower.includes("429") || lower.includes("rate limit") || lower.includes("too many requests")) {
    39→		return "Rate limited by the provider. Wait a moment and try again.";
    40→	}
    41→	if (lower.includes("timeout") || lower.includes("timed out")) {
    42→		return "Connection timed out. Check your endpoint URL and try again.";
    43→	}
    44→	if (lower.includes("connection refused") || lower.includes("econnrefused")) {
    45→		return "Connection refused. Make sure the provider endpoint is running and reachable.";
    46→	}
    47→	if (lower.includes("dns") || lower.includes("getaddrinfo") || lower.includes("name or service not known")) {
    48→		return "Could not resolve the endpoint address. Check the URL and try again.";
    49→	}
    50→	if (lower.includes("ollama pull")) {
    51→		return error;
    52→	}
    53→	if (lower.includes("404") || lower.includes("not found")) {
    54→		return "Model not found at this endpoint. Make sure it is installed and try again.";
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	return error;
    58→}
    59→
    60→export function isModelServiceNotConfigured(error) {
    61→	if (!error || typeof error !== "string") return false;
    62→	return error.toLowerCase().includes(MODEL_SERVICE_NOT_CONFIGURED);
    63→}
    64→
    65→export function isTimeoutError(error) {
    66→	if (!error || typeof error !== "string") return false;
    67→	var lower = error.toLowerCase();
    68→	return lower.includes("timeout") || lower.includes("timed out");
    69→}
    70→
    71→/**
    72→ * Validate provider credentials without saving them.
    73→ * Returns { valid, models?, error? }.
    74→ */
    75→export async function validateProviderKey(provider, apiKey, baseUrl, model, requestId) {
    76→	var payload = { provider, apiKey };
    77→	if (baseUrl) payload.baseUrl = baseUrl;
    78→	if (model) payload.model = model;
    79→	if (requestId) payload.requestId = requestId;
    80→
    81→	var res = await sendRpc("providers.validate_key", payload);
    82→	if (!res?.ok) {
    83→		return {
    84→			valid: false,
    85→			error: humanizeProbeError(res?.error?.serverMessage || res?.error?.message || "Failed to validate credentials."),
    86→		};
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	var data = res.payload || {};
    90→	if (data.valid) {
    91→		return { valid: true, models: data.models || [] };
    92→	}
    93→	return {
    94→		valid: false,
    95→		error: humanizeProbeError(data.error || "Validation failed."),
    96→	};
    97→}
    98→
    99→/**
   100→ * Test a single model from the live registry.
   101→ * Returns { ok, error? }.
   102→ */
   103→export async function testModel(modelId) {
   104→	for (var attempt = 0; attempt < MODEL_TEST_RETRY_ATTEMPTS; attempt++) {
   105→		var res = await sendRpc("models.test", { modelId });
   106→		if (res?.ok) {
   107→			return { ok: true };
   108→		}
   109→
   110→		var message = res?.error?.serverMessage || res?.error?.message || "Model test failed.";
   111→		var lower = String(message).toLowerCase();
   112→		var shouldRetry = lower.includes(MODEL_SERVICE_NOT_CONFIGURED) && attempt < MODEL_TEST_RETRY_ATTEMPTS - 1;
   113→
   114→		if (!shouldRetry) {
   115→			return {
   116→				ok: false,
   117→				error: humanizeProbeError(message),
   118→			};
   119→		}
   120→
   121→		await new Promise((resolve) => {
   122→			window.setTimeout(resolve, MODEL_TEST_RETRY_DELAY_MS);
   123→		});
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	return {
   127→		ok: false,
   128→		error: humanizeProbeError("Model test failed."),
   129→	};
   130→}
   131→
   132→/**
   133→ * Build the payload for a `providers.save_key` RPC call.
   134→ */
   135→export function buildSaveKeyPayload(providerName, apiKey, baseUrl, model) {
   136→	var payload = { provider: providerName, apiKey };
   137→	if (baseUrl) payload.baseUrl = baseUrl;
   138→	if (model) payload.model = model;
   139→	return payload;
   140→}
   141→
   142→/**
   143→ * Persist provider credentials via the `providers.save_key` RPC.
   144→ * Returns the RPC response (check `.ok` for success).
   145→ */
   146→export function saveProviderKey(providerName, apiKey, baseUrl, model) {
   147→	var payload = buildSaveKeyPayload(providerName, apiKey, baseUrl, model);
   148→	return sendRpc("providers.save_key", payload);
   149→}
   150→
   151→export async function validateProviderConnection(providerName) {
   152→	var res = await sendRpc("models.detect_supported", {
   153→		provider: providerName,
   154→		reason: "provider_credentials_validation",
   155→	});
   156→
   157→	if (!res?.ok) {
   158→		return {
   159→			ok: false,
   160→			message: res?.error?.serverMessage || res?.error?.message || "Failed to validate provider credentials.",
   161→		};
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	var payload = res.payload || {};
   165→	var total = payload.total || 0;
   166→	var supported = payload.supported || 0;
   167→	var unsupported = payload.unsupported || 0;
   168→	var errors = payload.errors || 0;
   169→
   170→	if (supported > 0) {
   171→		return {
   172→			ok: true,
   173→			message: null,
   174→		};
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// No probe targets usually means no model is configured yet.
   178→	if (total === 0) {
   179→		return {
   180→			ok: true,
   181→			message: null,
   182→		};
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	var reason = firstProbeFailure(payload);
   186→	if (!reason) {
   187→		reason = `0/${total} models responded successfully (unsupported: ${unsupported}, errors: ${errors}).`;
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	return {
   191→		ok: false,
   192→		message: `Credentials were saved, but validation failed: ${reason}`,
   193→	};
   194→}
   195→

     1→import { onEvent } from "./events.js";
     2→
     3→export var VALIDATION_HINT_TEXT = "Validation can take up to 30 seconds for some providers.";
     4→export var VALIDATION_HINT_RUNNING_TEXT = "Validating models... this can take up to 30 seconds.";
     5→
     6→var VALIDATION_PROGRESS_EVENT = "providers.validate.progress";
     7→
     8→function normalizeAttempt(value, fallback) {
     9→	if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return fallback;
    10→	return Math.max(1, Math.floor(value));
    11→}
    12→
    13→function stripModelNamespace(modelId) {
    14→	if (!modelId || typeof modelId !== "string") return modelId;
    15→	var sep = modelId.lastIndexOf("::");
    16→	return sep >= 0 ? modelId.slice(sep + 2) : modelId;
    17→}
    18→
    19→// biome-ignore lint/complexity/noExcessiveCognitiveComplexity: maps backend validation phases to progress UI updates.
    20→function progressFromValidationEvent(payload) {
    21→	if (!payload?.phase) return null;
    22→	var phase = payload.phase;
    23→	if (phase === "start") {
    24→		return { value: 8, message: payload.message || "Starting provider validation..." };
    25→	}
    26→	if (phase === "candidates_discovered") {
    27→		var count = Number.isFinite(payload.modelCount) ? payload.modelCount : null;
    28→		var message = count == null ? "Discovered candidate models." : `Discovered ${count} candidate models.`;
    29→		return { value: 24, message };
    30→	}
    31→	if (phase === "probe_started" || phase === "probe_failed" || phase === "probe_timeout") {
    32→		var total = normalizeAttempt(payload.totalAttempts, 1);
    33→		var attempt = Math.min(normalizeAttempt(payload.attempt, 1), total);
    34→		var value = 24 + (attempt / total) * 62;
    35→		var modelName = stripModelNamespace(payload.modelId);
    36→		var defaultMessage = modelName
    37→			? `Probing ${modelName} (${attempt}/${total})...`
    38→			: `Probing model ${attempt}/${total}...`;
    39→		return {
    40→			value,
    41→			message: payload.message || defaultMessage,
    42→		};
    43→	}
    44→	if (phase === "probe_succeeded") {
    45→		return { value: 94, message: payload.message || "Model probe succeeded." };
    46→	}
    47→	if (phase === "complete") {
    48→		return { value: 100, message: payload.message || "Validation complete." };
    49→	}
    50→	if (phase === "error") {
    51→		return { value: 98, message: payload.message || "Validation failed." };
    52→	}
    53→	return null;
    54→}
    55→
    56→export function clampValidationProgressPercent(value) {
    57→	if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;
    58→	return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value));
    59→}
    60→
    61→export function createValidationRequestId() {
    62→	var nonce = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
    63→	return `validate-${Date.now()}-${nonce}`;
    64→}
    65→
    66→export function subscribeValidationProgress(requestId, onProgress) {
    67→	if (!(requestId && typeof onProgress === "function")) {
    68→		return () => undefined;
    69→	}
    70→	var off = onEvent(VALIDATION_PROGRESS_EVENT, (payload) => {
    71→		if (!payload || payload.requestId !== requestId) return;
    72→		var update = progressFromValidationEvent(payload);
    73→		if (!update) return;
    74→		onProgress(update, payload);
    75→	});
    76→	return () => {
    77→		off();
    78→	};
    79→}
    80→

   246→function resetValidationProgress(state) {
   247→	if (!state) return;
   248→	state.value = 0;
   249→	state.progress.classList.remove("indeterminate");
   250→	state.progressBar.style.width = "0%";
   251→	state.progressText.textContent = VALIDATION_HINT_TEXT;
   252→}
   253→
   254→function completeValidationProgress(state, text) {
   255→	if (!state) return;
   256→	setValidationProgress(state, 100, text || "Validation complete.");
   257→}
   258→
   259→function createValidationRequestId() {
   260→	var nonce = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
   261→	return `validate-${Date.now()}-${nonce}`;
   262→}
   263→
   264→function normalizeAttempt(value, fallback) {
   265→	if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return fallback;
   266→	return Math.max(1, Math.floor(value));
   267→}
   268→
   269→// biome-ignore lint/complexity/noExcessiveCognitiveComplexity: maps backend validation phases to progress UI updates.
   270→function progressFromValidationEvent(payload) {
   271→	if (!payload?.phase) return null;
   272→	var phase = payload.phase;
   273→	if (phase === "start") {
   274→		return { value: 8, message: payload.message || "Starting provider validation..." };
   275→	}
   276→	if (phase === "candidates_discovered") {
   277→		var count = Number.isFinite(payload.modelCount) ? payload.modelCount : null;
   278→		var message = count == null ? "Discovered candidate models." : `Discovered ${count} candidate models.`;
   279→		return { value: 24, message };
   280→	}
   281→	if (phase === "probe_started" || phase === "probe_failed" || phase === "probe_timeout") {
   282→		var total = normalizeAttempt(payload.totalAttempts, 1);
   283→		var attempt = Math.min(normalizeAttempt(payload.attempt, 1), total);
   284→		var value = 24 + (attempt / total) * 62;
   285→		var modelName = stripModelNamespace(payload.modelId);
   286→		var defaultMessage = modelName
   287→			? `Probing ${modelName} (${attempt}/${total})...`
   288→			: `Probing model ${attempt}/${total}...`;
   289→		return {
   290→			value,
   291→			message: payload.message || defaultMessage,
   292→		};
   293→	}
   294→	if (phase === "probe_succeeded") {
   295→		return { value: 94, message: payload.message || "Model probe succeeded." };
   296→	}
   297→	if (phase === "complete") {
   298→		return { value: 100, message: payload.message || "Validation complete." };
   299→	}
   300→	if (phase === "error") {
   301→		return { value: 98, message: payload.message || "Validation failed." };
   302→	}
   303→	return null;
   304→}
   305→
   306→function bindValidationProgressEvents(state, requestId) {
   307→	if (!(state && requestId)) return () => undefined;
   308→	var off = onEvent(VALIDATION_PROGRESS_EVENT, (payload) => {
   309→		if (!payload || payload.requestId !== requestId) return;
   310→		var update = progressFromValidationEvent(payload);
   311→		if (!update) return;
   312→		setValidationProgress(state, update.value, update.message);
   313→	});
   314→	return () => {
   315→		off();
   316→	};
   317→}

        \"modelId\": probe_model.id,\n                            \"elapsedMs\": elapsed_ms,\n                            \"attempt\": attempt + 1,\n                            \"totalAttempts\": total_probe_attempts,\n                            \"unsupported\": is_unsupported,\n                            \"message\": detail.clone(),\n                        })),\n                    )\n                    .await;\n                    if is_unsupported {\n                        unsupported_errors.push(detail);\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    last_error = Some(detail);\n                    break;\n                },\n                Err(_) => {\n                    timeout_count += 1;\n                    let elapsed_ms = probe_started.elapsed().as_millis();\n                    warn!(\n                        provider = %validation_provider_name,\n                        model = %probe_model.id,\n                        timeout_count,\n                        max_timeouts = VALIDATION_MAX_TIMEOUTS,\n                        elapsed_ms,\n                        \"provider validation model probe timed out\"\n                    );\n                    self.emit_validation_progress(\n                        &validation_provider_name,\n                        request_id.as_deref(),\n                        \"probe_timeout\",\n                        progress_payload(serde_json::json!({\n                            \"modelId\": probe_model.id,\n                            \"elapsedMs\": elapsed_ms,\n                            \"attempt\": attempt + 1,\n                            \"totalAttempts\": total_probe_attempts,\n                            \"timeoutCount\": timeout_count,\n                            \"maxTimeouts\": VALIDATION_MAX_TIMEOUTS,\n                            \"message\": format!(\n                                \"Timed out probing model after {VALIDATION_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SECS} seconds.\"\n                            ),\n                        })),\n                    )\n                    .await;\n                    if timeout_count >= VALIDATION_MAX_TIMEOUTS {\n                        last_error = Some(format!(\n                            \"Connection timed out after {VALIDATION_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SECS} seconds while validating models. Check your endpoint URL and try again.\"\n                        ));\n                        break;\n                    }\n                    continue;\n                },\n            }\n        }\n\n        if probe_succeeded {\n            // Build model list for the frontend, excluding non-chat models.\n            let model_list: Vec<Value> = models\n                .iter()\n                .filter(|m| moltis_providers::is_chat_capable_model(&m.id))\n                .map(|m| {\n                    let supports_tools =\n                        temp_registry.get(&m.id).is_some_and(|p| p.supports_tools());\n                    serde_json::json!({\n                        \"id\": m.id,\n                        \"displayName\": m.display_name,\n                        \"provider\": m.provider,\n                        \"supportsTools\": supports_tools,\n                    })\n                })\n                .collect();\n\n            self.emit_validation_progress(\n                &validation_provider_name,\n                request_id.as_deref(),\n                \"complete\",\n                progress_payload(serde_json::json!({\n                    \"message\": \"Validation complete.\",\n                    \"modelCount\": model_list.len(),\n                })),\n            )\n            .await;\n            return Ok(serde_json::json!({\n                \"valid\": true,\n                \"models\": model_list,\n            }));\n        }\n\n        if !probe_attempted {\n            let error = \"Could not instantiate provider for probing.\";\n            self.emit_validation_progress(\n                &validation_provider_name,\n                request_id.as_deref(),\n                \"error\",\n                progress_payload(serde_json::json!({\n                    \"message\": error,\n                })),\n            )\n            .await;\n            return Ok(serde_json::json!({\n                \"valid\": false,\n                \"error\": error,\n            }));\n        }\n\n        if let Some(error) = last_error {\n            self.emit_validation_progress(\n                &validation_provider_name,\n                request_id.as_deref(),\n                \"error\",\n                progress_payload(serde_json::json!({\n                    \"message\": error,\n                })),\n            )\n            .await;\n            return Ok(serde_json::json!({\n                \"valid\": false,\n                \"error\": error,\n            }));\n        }\n\n        let unsupported_error = unsupported_errors.into_iter().next().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n            \"No supported chat models were found for this provider.\".to_string()\n        });\n        self.emit_validation_progress(\n            &validation_provider_name,\n            request_id.as_deref(),\n            \"error\",\n            progress_payload(serde_json::json!({\n                \"message\": unsupported_error.clone(),\n            })),\n        )\n        .await;\n        Ok(serde_json::json!({\n            \"valid\": false,\n            \"error\": unsupported_error,\n        }))",
  "new_string": "        info!(\n            provider = %validation_provider_name,\n            model_count = models.len(),\n            \"provider validation discovered candidate models\"\n        );\n\n        // Skip probing — the model list endpoint already proves the endpoint\n        // is reachable and any API key is valid.  Probing each model via a\n        // chat completion forces local LLM servers (Ollama, LemonadeSDK, …)\n        // to load/unload every model, causing enormous I/O for large model\n        // sets.  Errors will surface at chat time with actionable messages.\n        // Users can manually test individual models from the Providers page.\n        let model_list: Vec<Value> = models\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|m| moltis_providers::is_chat_capable_model(&m.id))\n            .map(|m| {\n                let supports_tools =\n                    temp_registry.get(&m.id).is_some_and(|p| p.supports_tools());\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"id\": m.id,\n                    \"displayName\": m.display_name,\n                    \"provider\": m.provider,\n                    \"supportsTools\": supports_tools,\n                })\n            })\n            .collect();\n\n        self.emit_validation_progress(\n            &validation_provider_name,\n            request_id.as_deref(),\n            \"complete\",\n            progress_payload(serde_json::json!({\n                \"message\": \"Validation complete.\",\n                \"modelCount\": model_list.len(),\n            })),\n        )\n        .await;\n        Ok(serde_json::json!({\n            \"valid\": true,\n            \"models\": model_list,\n        }))"
}

   222→function ProviderSection(props) {

   225→	var hasMore = group.models.length > DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MODELS;

   229→	function onDeleteProvider() {
   230→		if (deletingProvider.value) return;
   231→		requestConfirm(t("providers:removeProviderConfirm", { name: group.providerDisplayName })).then((yes) => {
   232→			if (!yes) return;
   233→			deletingProvider.value = group.provider;
   234→			providerActionError.value = "";
   235→			sendRpc("providers.remove_key", { provider: group.provider })
   236→				.then((res) => {
   237→					if (res?.ok) {
   238→						configuredModels.value = configuredModels.value.filter((entry) => entry.provider !== group.provider);
   239→						fetchModels();
   240→						fetchProviders();
   241→						return;
   242→					}
   243→					providerActionError.value = res?.error?.message || t("providers:failedToDeleteProvider");
   244→				})
   245→				.catch(() => {
   246→					providerActionError.value = t("providers:failedToDeleteProvider");
   247→				})
   248→				.finally(() => {
   249→					deletingProvider.value = "";
   250→				});
   251→		});
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	function onToggleModel(model) {
   255→		var method = model.disabled ? "models.enable" : "models.disable";
   256→		sendRpc(method, { modelId: model.id }).then((res) => {
   257→			if (res?.ok) {
   258→				providerActionError.value = "";
   259→				configuredModels.value = configuredModels.value.map((entry) =>
   260→					entry.id === model.id ? { ...entry, disabled: !model.disabled } : entry,
   261→				);
   262→				fetchModels();
   263→				fetchProviders();
   264→			} else {
   265→				providerActionError.value = res?.error?.message || t("providers:failedToUpdateModel");
   266→			}
   267→		});
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	function onSelectModels() {
   271→		openModelSelectorForProvider(group.provider, group.providerDisplayName);
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	return html`<div id=${`provider-${group.provider}`} class="max-w-form py-1">
   275→		<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
   276→			<div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
   277→				<h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--text-strong)] truncate">${group.providerDisplayName}</h3>
   278→				<span class="provider-item-badge ${group.authType}">
   279→					${group.authType === "oauth" ? t("providers:oauth") : group.authType === "local" ? t("providers:local") : t("providers:apiKey")}
   280→				</span>
   281→			</div>
   282→			<div class="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
   283→				${group.models.length > 0 ? html`<button class="provider-btn provider-btn-secondary provider-btn-sm" onClick=${onSelectModels}>${t("providers:preferredModels.button")}</button>` : null}
   284→				<button
   285→					class="provider-btn provider-btn-danger provider-btn-sm"
   286→					disabled=${deletingProvider.value === group.provider}
   287→					onClick=${onDeleteProvider}
   288→				>
   289→					${deletingProvider.value === group.provider ? t("common:status.deleting") : t("common:actions.delete")}
   290→				</button>
   291→			</div>
   292→		</div>
   293→		<div class="mt-2 border-b border-[var(--border)]"></div>
   294→		${
   295→			group.models.length === 0
   296→				? html`<div class="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">${t("providers:noActiveModels")}</div>`
   297→				: html`<div class="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
   298→					${visibleModels.map(
   299→						(model) => html`<div key=${model.id} class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 py-1">
   300→							<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
   301→								<div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
   302→									<div class="text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-strong)] truncate">${model.displayName || model.id}</div>
   303→									${model.preferred ? html`<span class="recommended-badge">${t("providers:preferred")}</span>` : null}
   304→									${model.unsupported ? html`<span class="provider-item-badge warning" title=${model.unsupportedReason || t("providers:modelNotSupported")}>${t("providers:unsupported")}</span>` : null}
   305→									${model.supportsTools ? null : html`<span class="provider-item-badge warning">${t("providers:chatOnly")}</span>`}
   306→									${model.disabled ? html`<span class="provider-item-badge muted">${t("providers:disabled")}</span>` : null}
   307→								</div>
   308→								<div class="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] font-mono opacity-75">${model.id}</div>
   309→								${model.unsupported && model.unsupportedReason ? html`<div class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--danger,#ef4444)]">${model.unsupportedReason}</div>` : null}
   310→								${model.createdAt ? html`<time class="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] opacity-60 block" data-epoch-ms=${model.createdAt * 1000} data-format="year-month"></time>` : null}
   311→							</div>
   312→							<button class="provider-btn provider-btn-secondary provider-btn-sm" onClick=${() => onToggleModel(model)}>
   313→								${model.disabled ? t("common:actions.enable") : t("common:actions.disable")}
   314→							</button>
   315→						</div>`,

   317→					${

   321→						onClick=${() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
   322→					>${expanded ? t("providers:showFewerModels") : t("providers:showAllModels", { count: hiddenCount })}</button>`
   323→							: null
   324→					}
   325→				</div>`

   327→	</div>`;

  4409→    async fn validate_key_ollama_model_probe_uses_v1_endpoint() {
  4410→        use axum::{
  4411→            Json, Router,

  4415→        let app = Router::new()

  4423→                    }))

  2790→
  2791→/// Reorder models so that known-fast, reliable models appear first for
  2792→/// validation probing.  We only need *one* successful response to prove the
  2793→/// API key works, so prefer the cheapest/fastest endpoints.
  2794→fn reorder_models_for_validation(models: &mut [moltis_providers::ModelInfo]) {
  2795→    /// Known-fast model substrings, ordered by preference.
  2796→    /// These are small/cheap models that respond quickly on every major provider.
  2797→    const FAST_PATTERNS: &[&str] = &[
  2798→        "highspeed",
  2799→        "gpt-4o-mini",
  2800→        "gpt-4.1-mini",
  2801→        "gpt-4.1-nano",
  2802→        "claude-3-haiku",
  2803→        "claude-3.5-haiku",
  2804→        "gemini-2.0-flash",
  2805→        "gemini-flash",
  2806→        "llama-3",
  2807→        "mistral-small",
  2808→        "deepseek-chat",
  2809→    ];
  2810→
  2811→    /// Known-slow or experimental model substrings to deprioritize.
  2812→    const SLOW_PATTERNS: &[&str] = &["search-preview", "seed-", "preview", "experimental"];
  2813→
  2814→    models.sort_by(|a, b| {
  2815→        let a_rank = probe_priority_rank(&a.id, FAST_PATTERNS, SLOW_PATTERNS);
  2816→        let b_rank = probe_priority_rank(&b.id, FAST_PATTERNS, SLOW_PATTERNS);
  2817→        a_rank.cmp(&b_rank)
  2818→    });
  2819→}
  2820→
  2821→fn probe_priority_rank(model_id: &str, fast: &[&str], slow: &[&str]) -> u8 {
  2822→    let raw = raw_model_id(model_id);
  2823→    for pattern in fast {
  2824→        if raw.contains(pattern) {
  2825→            return 0; // probe first
  2826→        }
  2827→    }
  2828→    for pattern in slow {
  2829→        if raw.contains(pattern) {
  2830→            return 2; // probe last
  2831→        }
  2832→    }
  2833→    1 // default: middle tier
  2834→}
  2835→

  2838→mod tests {

We only need *one* successful response to prove the\n/// API key works, so prefer the cheapest/fastest endpoints.\nfn reorder_models_for_validation(models: &mut [moltis_providers::ModelInfo]) {\n    /// Known-fast model substrings, ordered by preference.\n    /// These are small/cheap models that respond quickly on every major provider.\n    const FAST_PATTERNS: &[&str] = &[\n        \"highspeed\",\n        \"gpt-4o-mini\",\n        \"gpt-4.1-mini\",\n        \"gpt-4.1-nano\",\n        \"claude-3-haiku\",\n        \"claude-3.5-haiku\",\n        \"gemini-2.0-flash\",\n        \"gemini-flash\",\n        \"llama-3\",\n        \"mistral-small\",\n        \"deepseek-chat\",\n    ];\n\n    /// Known-slow or experimental model substrings to deprioritize.\n    const SLOW_PATTERNS: &[&str] = &[\"search-preview\", \"seed-\", \"preview\", \"experimental\"];\n\n    models.sort_by(|a, b| {\n        let a_rank = probe_priority_rank(&a.id, FAST_PATTERNS, SLOW_PATTERNS);\n        let b_rank = probe_priority_rank(&b.id, FAST_PATTERNS, SLOW_PATTERNS);\n        a_rank.cmp(&b_rank)\n    });\n}\n\nfn probe_priority_rank(model_id: &str, fast: &[&str], slow: &[&str]) -> u8 {\n    let raw = raw_model_id(model_id);\n    for pattern in fast {\n        if raw.contains(pattern) {\n            return 0; // probe first\n        }\n    }\n    for pattern in slow {\n        if raw.contains(pattern) {\n            return 2; // probe last\n        }\n    }\n    1 // default: middle tier\n}",
  "new_string": ""
}

    36→	for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {

> AGENT

Here's a summary of all changes:

**Backend (`crates/provider-setup/src/lib.rs`):**
- Removed the entire probe loop from `validate_key` (lines 2593-2835 → ~250 lines deleted)
- After discovering models from the provider registry, return `{ valid: true, models: [...] }` immediately
- Removed `reorder_models_for_validation()` and `probe_priority_rank()` dead code
- Removed 3 associated unit tests, renamed 2 test functions for accuracy
- All 90 remaining tests pass

**Frontend - Provider setup:**
- `providers.js`: "Save & Validate" → "Save", "Validating..." → "Saving...", removed 30-second timeout hints
- `onboarding-view.js`: Same button text updates
- `provider-validation-progress.js`: Updated hint text

**Frontend - Test button (`page-providers.js`):**
- Added `testModel` import from `provider-validation.js`
- Added `testingProvider` and `testResult` signals
- Added `onTestProvider()` handler with confirmation dialog
- Added "Test" button per provider section (uses existing `models.test` RPC)
- Shows success/error result inline after test

**i18n (en/fr/zh):**
- Added: `test`, `testing`, `testSuccess`, `testFailed`, `testProviderConfirm`
- Updated: `saveAndValidate`, `validating` to reflect "Save" instead of "Validate"

**E2E tests:**
- Updated "Save & Validate" button references to "Save" in `providers.spec.js` and `onboarding-anthropic.spec.js`

**Tailwind CSS:** Rebuilt to include the new `text-[var(--success,...)]` class.

}

Body: <a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=7" align="top"></a> **Stale test result persists after provider reconfiguration**

`testResult` is a module-level signal that is never cleared when a provider is reconfigured (key changed, deleted, re-added). If a user tests provider A successfully, reconfigures it with a new (potentially invalid) key, and returns to the page, the old "Connection successful." banner will still render until they click Test again. Consider clearing `testResult` (or the relevant entry) in the delete/save handler so the displayed result always reflects the current credentials.
